1→"""Direct tests for scan plan reconciliation orchestration.
     2→
     3→Tests exercise the real reconciliation logic with realistic plan and state
     4→data structures, mocking only at I/O boundaries (load_plan, save_plan).

     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from types import SimpleNamespace
    11→
    12→import pytest
    13→
    14→import desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_reconcile as reconcile_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan

    17→    StaleDimensionSyncResult,

    22→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23→# Helpers
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→
    26→def _runtime(*, state=None, config=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    27→    return SimpleNamespace(
    28→        state=state or {},
    29→        state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"),
    30→        config=config or {},
    31→    )
    32→
    33→
    34→def _make_state(
    35→    *,
    36→    issues: dict | None = None,
    37→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    38→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    39→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    40→    verified_strict_score: float | None = None,
    41→    scan_count: int = 1,
    42→) -> dict:
    43→    """Build a minimal but realistic state dict."""
    44→    state: dict = {
    45→        "issues": issues or {},
    46→        "scan_count": scan_count,
    47→        "dimension_scores": {},
    48→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    49→    }
    50→    if overall_score is not None:
    51→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    52→    if objective_score is not None:
    53→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    54→    if strict_score is not None:
    55→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    56→    if verified_strict_score is not None:
    57→        state["verified_strict_score"] = verified_strict_score
    58→    return state
    59→
    60→
    61→def _make_issue(
    62→    detector: str = "complexity",
    63→    file: str = "src/app.py",
    64→    status: str = "open",
    65→    **extra,
    66→) -> dict:
    67→    return {"detector": detector, "file": file, "status": status, **extra}

   174→class TestApplyPlanReconciliation:
   175→
   176→    def test_supersedes_resolved_issue(self):
   177→        """An issue in queue_order that becomes resolved should be superseded."""
   178→        plan = empty_plan()
   179→        plan["queue_order"] = ["issue-1", "issue-2"]
   180→        plan["overrides"] = {"issue-1": {"issue_id": "issue-1"}}
   181→        state = _make_state(issues={
   182→            "issue-1": _make_issue(status="resolved"),
   183→            "issue-2": _make_issue(status="open"),
   184→        })
   185→        from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan
   186→        result = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
   187→        assert "issue-1" in result.superseded
   188→        assert "issue-1" in plan["superseded"]
   189→        assert "issue-1" not in plan["queue_order"]
   190→        assert "issue-2" in plan["queue_order"]
   191→
   192→    def test_supersedes_disappeared_issue(self):
   193→        """An issue in queue_order that no longer exists should be superseded."""
   194→        plan = empty_plan()

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — sync plan queue metadata after a scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    10→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    11→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    14→    append_log_entry,
    15→    auto_cluster_issues,
    16→    load_plan,
    17→    reconcile_plan_after_scan,
    18→    save_plan,
    19→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    20→    sync_create_plan_needed,

    28→
    29→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    30→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    31→    return bool(
    32→        plan.get("queue_order")
    33→        or plan.get("overrides")
    34→        or plan.get("clusters")
    35→        or plan.get("skipped")
    36→    )
    37→
    38→
    39→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    40→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    41→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    42→        return False
    43→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    44→    if recon.resurfaced:
    45→        print(
    46→            colorize(
    47→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    48→                "cyan",
    49→            )
    50→        )
    51→    return bool(recon.changes)
    52→
    53→
    54→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    55→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
    56→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    57→    if sync.injected:
    58→        print(
    59→            colorize(
    60→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
    61→                "cyan",
    62→            )
    63→        )
    64→    return bool(sync.changes)
    65→
    66→
    67→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    68→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
    69→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    70→    if sync.pruned:
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
    74→                "cyan",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→    if sync.injected:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
    81→                "cyan",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→    return bool(sync.changes)
    85→
    86→
    87→def _sync_auto_clusters(
    88→    plan: dict[str, object],
    89→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    90→    *,
    91→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    92→    policy=None,
    93→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    94→) -> bool:
    95→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
    96→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
    97→        plan, state,
    98→        target_strict=target_strict,
    99→        policy=policy,
   100→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   101→    ))
   102→
   103→
   104→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   105→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   106→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   107→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   108→        return False
   109→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   110→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   111→        return False
   112→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   113→    if scores.strict is None:
   114→        return False
   115→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   116→        "strict": scores.strict,
   117→        "overall": scores.overall,
   118→        "objective": scores.objective,
   119→        "verified": scores.verified,
   120→    }
   121→    return True
   122→
   123→
   124→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   125→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   126→) -> bool:
   127→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   128→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   129→        return False
   130→
   131→    try:
   132→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   133→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   134→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   135→            score_display_mode,
   136→        )
   137→
   138→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   139→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   140→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   141→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   142→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   143→        return False
   144→    if not queue_empty:
   145→        return False
   146→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   147→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   148→    return True
   149→
   150→
   151→def _subjective_policy_context(
   152→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   153→    plan: dict[str, object],
   154→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   155→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   156→    from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   157→
   158→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   159→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   160→        runtime.state,
   161→        target_strict=target_strict,
   162→        plan=plan,
   163→    )
   164→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   165→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   166→
   167→
   168→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   169→    plan: dict[str, object],
   170→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   171→) -> bool:
   172→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   173→    if changed:
   174→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   175→    return changed
   176→
   177→
   178→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   179→    plan: dict[str, object],
   180→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   181→    *,
   182→    policy,
   183→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   184→) -> bool:
   185→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   186→        plan,
   187→        state,
   188→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   189→            p,
   190→            s,
   191→            policy=policy,
   192→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   193→        ),
   194→    )
   195→    if changed:
   196→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   197→    return changed
   198→
   199→
   200→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   201→    plan: dict[str, object],
   202→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   203→    *,
   204→    target_strict: float,
   205→    policy,
   206→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   207→) -> bool:
   208→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   209→        plan,
   210→        state,
   211→        target_strict=target_strict,
   212→        policy=policy,
   213→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   214→    )
   215→    if changed:
   216→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   217→    return changed
   218→
   219→
   220→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   221→    plan: dict[str, object],
   222→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   223→) -> bool:
   224→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   225→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   226→        return False
   227→    if triage_sync.injected:
   228→        print(
   229→            colorize(
   230→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   231→                "cyan",
   232→            )
   233→        )
   234→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   235→    return True
   236→
   237→
   238→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   239→    plan: dict[str, object],
   240→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   241→    *,
   242→    policy,
   243→) -> bool:
   244→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   245→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   246→        return False
   247→    append_log_entry(
   248→        plan,
   249→        "sync_communicate_score",
   250→        actor="system",
   251→        detail={"injected": True},
   252→    )
   253→    return True
   254→
   255→
   256→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   257→    plan: dict[str, object],
   258→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   259→    *,
   260→    policy,
   261→) -> bool:
   262→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   263→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   264→        return False
   265→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   266→        print(
   267→            colorize(
   268→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   269→                "cyan",
   270→            )
   271→        )
   272→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   273→    return True
   274→
   275→
   276→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   277→    plan: dict[str, object],
   278→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   279→) -> bool:
   280→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   281→    if seeded:
   282→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   283→        return True
   284→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   285→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   286→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   287→    if cleared:
   288→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   289→    return cleared
   290→
   291→
   292→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   293→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   294→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   295→    try:
   296→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   297→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   298→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   299→        return
   300→    dirty = False
   301→
   302→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   303→        dirty = True
   304→
   305→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   306→        dirty = True
   307→
   308→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   309→        runtime,
   310→        plan,
   311→    )
   312→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   313→        plan,
   314→        runtime.state,
   315→        policy=policy,
   316→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   317→    ):
   318→        dirty = True
   319→
   320→    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   321→        plan,
   322→        runtime.state,
   323→        target_strict=target_strict,
   324→        policy=policy,
   325→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   326→    ):
   327→        dirty = True
   328→
   329→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   330→        dirty = True
   331→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   332→        dirty = True
   333→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   334→        dirty = True
   335→    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   336→        dirty = True
   337→
   338→    if dirty:
   339→        try:
   340→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   341→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   342→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   343→

1→"""Tests for shared queue selection in desloppify.work_queue."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import build_work_queue as _build_work_queue
     7→
     8→
     9→def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    10→    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))
    11→
    12→
    13→def _issue(
    14→    fid: str,
    15→    *,
    16→    detector: str = "smells",
    17→    file: str = "src/a.py",
    18→    tier: int = 3,
    19→    confidence: str = "medium",
    20→    status: str = "open",
    21→    detail: dict | None = None,
    22→) -> dict:
    23→    return {
    24→        "id": fid,
    25→        "detector": detector,
    26→        "file": file,
    27→        "tier": tier,
    28→        "confidence": confidence,
    29→        "summary": fid,
    30→        "status": status,
    31→        "detail": detail or {},
    32→    }
    33→
    34→
    35→def _state(issues: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    36→    return {
    37→        "issues": {f["id"]: f for f in issues},
    38→        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    39→    }
    40→
    41→
    42→def test_review_issue_uses_natural_tier():
    43→    review = _issue(
    44→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    45→        detector="review",
    46→        tier=2,
    47→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    48→    )
    49→    mechanical = _issue("smells::src/a.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
    50→    state = _state(
    51→        [review, mechanical],
    52→        dimension_scores={
    53→            "Naming quality": {"score": 94.0, "strict": 94.0, "failing": 1}
    54→        },
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
    58→    by_id = {item["id"]: item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "issue"}
    59→    assert "review::src/a.py::naming" in by_id
    60→    assert "smells::src/a.py::x" in by_id
    61→
    62→
    63→def test_review_items_ranked_alongside_mechanical():
    64→    urgent = _issue(
    65→        "security::src/a.py::x", detector="security", tier=1, confidence="high"
    66→    )
    67→    review = _issue(
    68→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    69→        detector="review",
    70→        tier=2,
    71→        confidence="high",
    72→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    73→    )
    74→    state = _state(
    75→        [urgent, review],
    76→        dimension_scores={
    77→            "Naming quality": {"score": 92.0, "strict": 92.0, "failing": 2}
    78→        },
    79→    )
    80→
    81→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
    82→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
    83→    # Both items appear in the queue
    84→    assert "security::src/a.py::x" in ids
    85→    assert "review::src/a.py::naming" in ids
    86→
    87→
    88→def test_review_items_sort_by_issue_weight_within_tier():
    89→    standard = _issue(
    90→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    91→        detector="review",
    92→        tier=2,
    93→        confidence="high",
    94→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    95→    )
    96→    holistic = _issue(
    97→        "review::src/a.py::logic",
    98→        detector="review",
    99→        tier=2,
   100→        confidence="high",
   101→        detail={"dimension": "logic_clarity", "holistic": True},
   102→    )
   103→    state = _state(
   104→        [standard, holistic],
   105→        dimension_scores={
   106→            "Naming quality": {"score": 92.0, "strict": 92.0, "failing": 2},
   107→            "Logic clarity": {"score": 88.0, "strict": 88.0, "failing": 3},
   108→        },
   109→    )
   110→
   111→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
   112→    # Within same tier and confidence, holistic (higher review_weight) sorts first
   113→    assert [item["id"] for item in queue["items"][:2]] == [
   114→        "review::src/a.py::logic",
   115→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
   116→    ]
   117→
   118→
   119→def test_queue_contains_mechanical_and_synthetic_subjective_items():
   120→    # When no objective backlog exists, stale subjective items appear alongside mechanical.
   121→    state = _state(
   122→        [],
   123→        dimension_scores={
   124→            "Naming quality": {"score": 94.0, "strict": 94.0, "failing": 2, "stale": True},
   125→            "Logic clarity": {"score": 100.0, "strict": 100.0, "failing": 0},
   126→        },
   127→    )
   128→
   129→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   130→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
   131→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in ids
   132→
   133→
   134→def test_subjective_items_do_not_starve_objective_queue_head():
   135→    """Non-initial subjective items are excluded when objective backlog exists."""
   136→    review = _issue(
   137→        "review::.::holistic::naming_quality::abc12345",
   138→        detector="review",
   139→        tier=3,
   140→        detail={"holistic": True, "dimension": "naming_quality"},
   141→    )
   142→    state = _state(
   143→        [
   144→            _issue("security::src/a.py::x", detector="security", tier=1, confidence="high"),
   145→            _issue("smells::src/a.py::y", detector="smells", tier=2, confidence="medium"),
   146→            review,
   147→        ],
   148→        dimension_scores={
   149→            "Naming quality": {"score": 92.0, "strict": 92.0, "failing": 3},
   150→        },
   151→    )
   152→
   153→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   154→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   155→    # Objective items present
   156→    assert "security::src/a.py::x" in ids
   157→    # Subjective items blocked while objective backlog exists
   158→    assert not any(item_id.startswith("subjective::") for item_id in ids)
   159→
   160→
   161→def test_impact_sort_with_count_limit():
   162→    """With count=1 and no dimension scores, tiebreakers apply: high
   163→    confidence mechanical leads."""
   164→    state = _state(
   165→        [
   166→            _issue("security::src/a.py::x", detector="security", tier=1, confidence="high"),
   167→        ],
   168→        dimension_scores={
   169→            "Naming quality": {"score": 80.0, "strict": 80.0, "failing": 5},
   170→        },
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=1, include_subjective=True)
   174→    assert queue["items"][0]["id"] == "security::src/a.py::x"
   175→
   176→
   177→def test_explain_payload_added_when_requested():
   178→    state = _state(
   179→        [
   180→            _issue(
   181→                "smells::src/a.py::x", tier=3, confidence="medium", detail={"count": 7}
   182→            )
   183→        ]
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, explain=True)
   187→    item = queue["items"][0]
   188→    assert "explain" in item
   189→    assert item["explain"]["ranking_factors"] == [
   190→        "estimated_impact desc",
   191→        "confidence asc",
   192→        "count desc",
   193→        "id asc",
   194→    ]
   195→    assert "estimated_impact" in item["explain"]
   196→
   197→
   198→def test_subjective_items_respect_target_threshold():
   199→    state = _state(
   200→        [],
   201→        dimension_scores={
   202→            "Naming quality": {"score": 94.0, "strict": 94.0, "failing": 2, "stale": True},
   203→            "AI generated debt": {"score": 96.0, "strict": 96.0, "failing": 1, "stale": True},
   204→        },
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    queue = build_work_queue(
   208→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=95
   209→    )

   211→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in ids
   212→    assert "subjective::ai_generated_debt" not in ids

   214→
   215→def test_subjective_item_uses_show_review_when_matching_review_issues_exist():
   216→    review = _issue(
   217→        "review::.::holistic::mid_level_elegance::split::abc12345",
   218→        detector="review",

   220→        detail={"holistic": True, "dimension": "mid_level_elegance"},

   240→def test_stale_subjective_item_uses_show_review_when_matching_review_issues_exist():

   242→        "review::.::holistic::initialization_coupling::abc12345",

   253→                "failing": 1,
   254→                "checks": 1,
   255→                "detectors": {
   256→                    "subjective_assessment": {

   258→                    }
   259→                },
   260→            },
   261→        },

   337→    assert opts.scope is None

   378→# ── Subjective threshold clamping ─────────────────────────
   379→
   380→
   381→def test_subjective_threshold_clamped_to_valid_range():
   382→    """Threshold values outside [0, 100] are clamped, not rejected."""
   383→    state = _state(
   384→        [],
   385→        dimension_scores={
   386→            "Naming quality": {"score": 50.0, "strict": 50.0, "failing": 1, "stale": True},
   387→        },
   388→    )
   389→    # threshold=-10 clamps to 0.0 -> score 50 >= 0 -> item excluded
   390→    queue = build_work_queue(
   391→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=-10
   392→    )
   393→    subj_items = [item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   394→    assert subj_items == []
   395→
   396→    # threshold=200 clamps to 100.0 -> score 50 < 100 -> item included
   397→    queue2 = build_work_queue(
   398→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=200
   399→    )
   400→    subj_items2 = [item for item in queue2["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   401→    assert len(subj_items2) >= 1
   402→

   530→# ── Subjective items appear alongside mechanical ──────────
   531→
   532→
   533→def test_stale_subjective_gated_when_objective_backlog_exists():
   534→    """Stale subjective items are suppressed while objective backlog exists."""
   535→    objective_issues = [
   536→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   537→        for c in "abcd"
   538→    ]
   539→    state = _state(
   540→        objective_issues,
   541→        dimension_scores={
   542→            "Naming quality": {
   543→                "score": 70.0,
   544→                "strict": 70.0,
   545→                "failing": 1,
   546→                "detectors": {
   547→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   548→                },
   549→            },
   550→        },
   551→    )
   552→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   553→        "naming_quality": {
   554→            "score": 70.0,
   555→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   556→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   557→        }
   558→    }
   559→
   560→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   561→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   562→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   563→
   564→    # Stale subjective items are gated when objective backlog > 0
   565→    assert len(subj_ids) == 0
   566→    assert len(ids) == 4  # 4 objective only
   567→

   599→
   600→def test_unassessed_subjective_visible_with_objective_backlog():
   601→    """When initial reviews exist, only they are shown — objective items hidden.
   602→
   603→    Initial reviews are the first phase: scan → review → score → plan.
   604→    Objective work becomes visible only after initial reviews are done.
   605→    """
   606→    objective_issues = [
   607→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   608→        for c in "abcd"
   609→    ]
   610→    state = _state(
   611→        objective_issues,
   612→        dimension_scores={
   613→            "Naming quality": {
   614→                "score": 0.0,
   615→                "strict": 0.0,
   616→                "failing": 0,
   617→                "detectors": {
   618→                    "subjective_assessment": {
   619→                        "dimension_key": "naming_quality",
   620→                        "placeholder": True,
   621→                    },
   622→                },
   623→            },
   624→        },
   625→    )
   626→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   627→        "naming_quality": {
   628→            "score": 0.0,
   629→            "placeholder": True,
   630→        }
   631→    }
   632→
   633→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   634→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   635→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   636→
   637→    # Initial reviews gate: only subjective items visible, objective hidden
   638→    assert len(subj_ids) == 1
   639→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in ids
   640→    assert len(ids) == 1  # only initial review — objective items hidden
   641→
   642→
   643→# ── Impact-based ordering ──────────────────────────────────
   644→
   645→
   646→def test_high_impact_subjective_before_low_impact_mechanical():
   647→    """A subjective dimension with high headroom sorts before a mechanical
   648→    issue in a near-perfect dimension."""
   649→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   650→
   651→    subj_item = {
   652→        "id": "subjective::naming",
   653→        "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   654→        "detail": {"dimension_name": "Naming quality", "strict_score": 43.0},
   655→        "confidence": "medium",
   656→    }
   657→    mech_item = {
   658→        "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   659→        "kind": "issue",
   660→        "detector": "smells",
   661→        "confidence": "high",
   662→        "detail": {"count": 1},
   663→    }
   664→
   665→    dimension_scores = {
   666→        "Naming quality": {
   667→            "score": 43.0,
   668→            "strict": 43.0,
   669→            "failing": 3,
   670→            "detectors": {
   671→                "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   672→            },
   673→        },
   674→        "Code quality": {
   675→            "score": 97.0,
   676→            "strict": 97.0,
   677→            "checks": 200,
   678→            "failing": 1,
   679→            "detectors": {
   680→                "smells": {"weighted_failures": 1, "potential": 200},
   681→            },
   682→        },
   683→    }
   684→
   685→    enrich_with_impact([subj_item, mech_item], dimension_scores)
   686→
   687→    # Naming quality has much more headroom (57) than Code quality (3)
   688→    assert subj_item["estimated_impact"] > mech_item["estimated_impact"]
   689→    assert item_sort_key(subj_item) < item_sort_key(mech_item)
   690→
   691→
   692→def test_impact_fallback_when_no_dimension_scores():
   693→    """When no dimension_scores exist, all items get impact 0.0 and
   694→    tiebreakers provide default ordering."""
   695→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   696→
   697→    items = [
   698→        {
   699→            "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   700→            "kind": "issue",
   701→            "detector": "smells",
   702→            "confidence": "high",
   703→            "detail": {"count": 5},
   704→        },
   705→        {
   706→            "id": "smells::src/b.py::y",
   707→            "kind": "issue",
   708→            "detector": "smells",
   709→            "confidence": "medium",
   710→            "detail": {"count": 2},
   711→        },
   712→    ]
   713→
   714→    enrich_with_impact(items, {})
   715→
   716→    assert all(item["estimated_impact"] == 0.0 for item in items)
   717→    # High confidence sorts before medium when impact is equal
   718→    assert item_sort_key(items[0]) < item_sort_key(items[1])
   719→
   720→
   721→# ── Evidence-only filtering ──────────────────────────────
   722→
   723→
   724→def test_low_confidence_props_excluded_from_queue():
   725→    """Low-confidence issues from detectors with standalone_threshold are filtered."""

   747→    )
   748→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)

   752→
   753→def test_detector_without_threshold_unaffected():
   754→    """Detectors without standalone_threshold (e.g. unused) pass at any confidence."""
   755→    state = _state(
   756→        [_issue("unused::src/a.py::x", detector="unused", confidence="low")]
   757→    )
   758→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)

   762→
   763→def test_evidence_only_issue_still_in_state():
   764→    """Evidence-only issues are filtered from queue but remain in state (scoring intact)."""
   765→    issues = [_issue("props::src/a.tsx::big", detector="props", confidence="low")]
   766→    state = _state(issues)
   767→    # Issue exists in state
   768→    assert "props::src/a.tsx::big" in state["issues"]
   769→    # But not in queue
   770→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
   771→    assert len(queue["items"]) == 0
   772→
   773→
   774→def test_evidence_only_items_dont_block_subjective():
   775→    """Evidence-only items (filtered before lifecycle) don't block subjective items.
   776→
   777→    The lifecycle filter operates on the actual queue contents after all prior
   778→    filters. If evidence-only items are the only objective issues and they get
   779→    filtered out, subjective items surface because nothing objective remains.
   780→    """
   781→    # All issues are low-confidence smells (below standalone_threshold)
   782→    issues = [
   783→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", confidence="low")
   784→        for c in "abcd"
   785→    ]
   786→    state = _state(
   787→        issues,
   788→        dimension_scores={
   789→            "Naming quality": {"score": 70.0, "strict": 70.0, "failing": 1, "stale": True},
   790→        },
   791→    )
   792→
   793→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   794→    subj_ids = [
   795→        item["id"] for item in queue["items"]
   796→        if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"
   797→    ]
   798→    # Evidence-only items filtered before lifecycle → no objective items remain
   799→    # → subjective items surface
   800→    assert len(subj_ids) == 1
   801→
   802→
   803→def test_impact_floor_filters_negligible_impact():
   804→    """Mechanical issues with estimated_impact < 0.05 are dropped."""
   805→    state = _state(
   806→        [_issue("unused::src/a.py::x", detector="unused", confidence="high")],
   807→        dimension_scores={
   808→            "Code quality": {"score": 99.99, "strict": 99.99, "checks": 10000, "failing": 1},
   809→        },
   810→    )
   811→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
   812→    # Impact is near-zero because dimension is at ceiling
   813→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
   814→    # The issue should be filtered by the impact floor
   815→    assert "unused::src/a.py::x" not in ids
   816→
   817→
   818→def test_impact_floor_preserves_items_without_scores():
   819→    """When no dimension_scores exist, items get impact 0.0 and are preserved."""
   820→    state = _state(
   821→        [_issue("unused::src/a.py::x", detector="unused", confidence="high")],
   822→        dimension_scores={},
   823→    )
   824→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
   825→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
   826→    assert "unused::src/a.py::x" in ids
   827→
   828→
   829→def test_registry_standalone_threshold_count():
   830→    """Exactly 7 detectors have standalone_threshold='medium'."""
   831→    from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
   832→
   833→    threshold_detectors = [
   834→        name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items()
   835→        if meta.standalone_threshold == "medium"
   836→    ]
   837→    assert sorted(threshold_detectors) == sorted([
   838→        "props", "patterns", "naming", "react", "smells", "dupes", "dict_keys",
   839→    ])

   841→
   842→# ── Cluster collapse ─────────────────────────────────────
   843→
   844→
   845→def test_collapse_clusters_preserves_order():
   846→    """Cluster meta-item appears at position of first member, not re-sorted."""
   847→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
   848→
   849→    plan: dict = {
   850→        "queue_order": [],
   851→        "skipped": {},
   852→        "overrides": {},
   853→        "clusters": {},
   854→        "active_cluster": None,
   855→    }
   856→    plan["clusters"]["auto/unused"] = {
   857→        "name": "auto/unused",
   858→        "auto": True,
   859→        "cluster_key": "auto::unused",
   860→        "issue_ids": ["u1", "u2"],
   861→        "description": "Remove 2 unused issues",
   862→        "action": "desloppify autofix unused-imports --dry-run",
   863→        "user_modified": False,
   864→    }
   865→
   866→    # Place a non-cluster item first, then the two cluster members
   867→    items = [
   868→        {"id": "other", "kind": "issue", "detector": "structural",
   869→         "confidence": "medium", "detail": {}},
   870→        {"id": "u1", "kind": "issue", "detector": "unused",
   871→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   872→        {"id": "u2", "kind": "issue", "detector": "unused",
   873→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   874→    ]
   875→
   876→    result = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   877→    # Non-cluster item stays at position 0
   878→    assert result[0]["id"] == "other"
   879→    # Cluster meta-item appears at position 1 (where first member was)
   880→    assert result[1]["kind"] == "cluster"
   881→    assert result[1]["id"] == "auto/unused"
   882→    assert len(result) == 2  # other + cluster
   883→
   884→
   885→# -- Plan-ordered subjective items surface despite objective backlog --------
   886→
   887→
   888→def test_plan_ordered_stale_subjective_gated_with_objective_backlog():
   889→    """Stale subjective items are gated by lifecycle filter while objective
   890→    issues exist, even when the plan includes them in queue_order.
   891→    """
   892→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   893→
   894→    objective_issues = [
   895→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   896→        for c in "abcd"
   897→    ]
   898→    state = _state(
   899→        objective_issues,
   900→        dimension_scores={
   901→            "Naming quality": {
   902→                "score": 70.0,
   903→                "strict": 70.0,
   904→                "failing": 1,
   905→                "detectors": {
   906→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   907→                },
   908→            },
   909→        },
   910→    )
   911→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   912→        "naming_quality": {
   913→            "score": 70.0,
   914→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   915→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   916→        }
   917→    }
   918→
   919→    # Without plan: stale subjective item is gated
   920→    queue_no_plan = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   921→    subj_no_plan = [
   922→        i["id"] for i in queue_no_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   923→    ]
   924→    assert len(subj_no_plan) == 0
   925→
   926→    # With plan that includes the stale dim in queue_order: still gated
   927→    plan = empty_plan()
   928→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   929→        "subjective::naming_quality",
   930→        "smells::src/a.py::x",
   931→        "smells::src/b.py::x",
   932→        "smells::src/c.py::x",
   933→        "smells::src/d.py::x",
   934→    ]
   935→    queue_with_plan = build_work_queue(
   936→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan,
   937→    )
   938→    subj_with_plan = [
   939→        i["id"] for i in queue_with_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   940→    ]
   941→    # Stale subjective items gated by lifecycle filter even with plan ordering
   942→    assert len(subj_with_plan) == 0
   943→
   944→
   945→# ── Lifecycle filter runs after plan_presort ───────────
   946→
   947→
   948→def test_skipped_objective_items_dont_block_subjective():
   949→    """Plan-skipped objective items are removed before lifecycle filter,
   950→    so they don't block subjective reassessment items.
   951→    """
   952→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   953→
   954→    objective_issues = [
   955→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   956→        for c in "abcd"
   957→    ]
   958→    state = _state(
   959→        objective_issues,
   960→        dimension_scores={
   961→            "Naming quality": {
   962→                "score": 70.0,
   963→                "strict": 70.0,
   964→                "failing": 1,
   965→                "detectors": {
   966→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   967→                },
   968→            },
   969→        },
   970→    )
   971→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   972→        "naming_quality": {
   973→            "score": 70.0,
   974→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   975→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   976→        }
   977→    }
   978→
   979→    # Skip ALL objective issues in the plan
   980→    plan = empty_plan()
   981→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   982→        "subjective::naming_quality",
   983→        "smells::src/a.py::x",
   984→        "smells::src/b.py::x",
   985→        "smells::src/c.py::x",
   986→        "smells::src/d.py::x",
   987→    ]
   988→    plan["skipped"] = {
   989→        "smells::src/a.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   990→        "smells::src/b.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   991→        "smells::src/c.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   992→        "smells::src/d.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   993→    }
   994→
   995→    queue = build_work_queue(
   996→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan,
   997→    )
   998→    subj_ids = [
   999→        i["id"] for i in queue["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
  1000→    ]
  1001→    # All objective items skipped → lifecycle filter sees no objective work
  1002→    # → stale subjective item surfaces
  1003→    assert len(subj_ids) == 1
  1004→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in subj_ids
  1005→

  1007→# ── Wontfix / resolved issues excluded (#193) ──────────
  1008→
  1009→
  1010→def test_wontfixed_issues_excluded_from_queue():
  1011→    """Issues with status 'wontfix' never appear in the default queue.
  1012→
  1013→    Regression test for #193: wontfixed issues were leaking into the
  1014→    auto-generated queue because filtering was spread across multiple
  1015→    modules and easy to miss.
  1016→    """
  1017→    state = _state(
  1018→        [
  1019→            _issue("a", status="open"),
  1020→            _issue("b", status="wontfix"),
  1021→            _issue("c", status="fixed"),
  1022→            _issue("d", status="open"),
  1023→        ]
  1024→    )
  1025→
  1026→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
  1027→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
  1028→    assert "a" in ids
  1029→    assert "d" in ids
  1030→    assert "b" not in ids  # wontfix excluded
  1031→    assert "c" not in ids  # fixed excluded
  1032→
  1033→
  1034→def test_wontfixed_issues_excluded_with_plan():
  1035→    """Wontfixed issues stay out even when a plan is active."""
  1036→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
  1037→
  1038→    plan = empty_plan()
  1039→    plan["queue_order"] = ["a", "b", "c", "d"]
  1040→
  1041→    state = _state(
  1042→        [
  1043→            _issue("a", status="open"),
  1044→            _issue("b", status="wontfix"),
  1045→            _issue("c", status="fixed"),
  1046→            _issue("d", status="open"),
  1047→        ]
  1048→    )
  1049→
  1050→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False, plan=plan)
  1051→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
  1052→    assert "a" in ids
  1053→    assert "d" in ids
  1054→    assert "b" not in ids
  1055→    assert "c" not in ids
  1056→

  1058→# ── Triage lifecycle ordering ────────────────────────────────
  1059→
  1060→
  1061→def test_triage_stages_hidden_during_initial_reviews():
  1062→    """Phase 1 hides triage stages and workflow actions — only initial reviews visible."""
  1063→    objective_issues = [
  1064→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
  1065→        for c in "ab"
  1066→    ]
  1067→    state = _state(
  1068→        objective_issues,
  1069→        dimension_scores={
  1070→            "Naming quality": {
  1071→                "score": 0.0,
  1072→                "strict": 0.0,
  1073→                "failing": 0,
  1074→                "detectors": {
  1075→                    "subjective_assessment": {
  1076→                        "dimension_key": "naming_quality",
  1077→                        "placeholder": True,
  1078→                    },
  1079→                },
  1080→            },
  1081→        },
  1082→    )
  1083→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
  1084→        "naming_quality": {"score": 0.0, "placeholder": True}
  1085→    }
  1086→
  1087→    # Inject a triage stage and a workflow action into queue_order so they
  1088→    # would appear if the lifecycle filter didn't hide them.
  1089→    plan = {
  1090→        "queue_order": [
  1091→            "triage::observe",
  1092→            "workflow::communicate-score",
  1093→            "subjective::naming_quality",
  1094→        ],
  1095→        "queue_skipped": {},
  1096→    }
  1097→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan)
  1098→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
  1099→
  1100→    # Only initial review visible — triage and workflow hidden
  1101→    assert ids == ["subjective::naming_quality"]
  1102→
  1103→
  1104→def test_triage_stages_sort_after_workflow_in_natural_ranking():
  1105→    """In natural ranking (no plan), workflow actions sort before triage stages."""
  1106→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
  1107→
  1108→    workflow_item = {
  1109→        "id": "workflow::communicate-score",
  1110→        "kind": "workflow_action",
  1111→        "tier": 1,
  1112→        "confidence": "high",
  1113→        "detector": "workflow",
  1114→        "file": ".",
  1115→    }
  1116→    triage_item = {
  1117→        "id": "triage::observe",
  1118→        "kind": "workflow_stage",
  1119→        "tier": 1,
  1120→        "confidence": "high",
  1121→        "detector": "triage",
  1122→        "file": ".",
  1123→        "detail": {"stage": "observe"},
  1124→        "is_blocked": False,
  1125→    }
  1126→
  1127→    wf_key = item_sort_key(workflow_item)
  1128→    tr_key = item_sort_key(triage_item)
  1129→    assert wf_key < tr_key, "workflow actions should sort before triage stages"
  1130→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from typing import Any, NotRequired, Required, TypedDict

    17→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",

    22→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):

    31→

    48→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters

    52→    optional: bool

    64→

   131→    triaged_ids: list[str]

   142→    last_triage: LastTriageSnapshot
   143→
   144→
   145→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   146→    version: Required[int]
   147→    created: Required[str]
   148→    updated: Required[str]
   149→    queue_order: list[str]
   150→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   151→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   152→    active_cluster: str | None
   153→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   154→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   155→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   156→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   157→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   158→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   159→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   160→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   161→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   162→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   163→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   164→
   165→
   166→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   167→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   168→    now = utc_now()
   169→    return {
   170→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   171→        "created": now,
   172→        "updated": now,
   173→        "queue_order": [],
   174→        "deferred": [],
   175→        "skipped": {},
   176→        "active_cluster": None,
   177→        "overrides": {},
   178→        "clusters": {},
   179→        "superseded": {},
   180→        "promoted_ids": [],
   181→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   182→        "execution_log": [],
   183→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   184→        "commit_log": [],
   185→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   186→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   191→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   192→
   193→    Runtime contract is v7-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   194→    """
   195→    defaults = empty_plan()
   196→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   197→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   198→    _upgrade_plan_to_v7(plan)
   199→
   200→
   201→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   202→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   203→    return {
   204→        name: cluster
   205→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   206→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   212→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   213→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   214→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   215→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   216→
   217→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   218→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   219→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   220→    if overlap:
   221→        raise ValueError(
   222→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   223→        )
   224→
   225→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   226→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   227→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   228→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   229→            raise ValueError(
   230→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   231→            )
   232→
   233→
   234→__all__ = [
   235→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   236→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   237→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   238→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   239→    "Cluster",
   240→    "CommitRecord",
   241→    "ItemOverride",
   242→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   243→    "PlanModel",
   244→    "PlanStartScores",
   245→    "SkipEntry",

   250→    "empty_plan",
   251→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   252→    "triage_clusters",
   253→    "validate_plan",
   254→]
   255→

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    11→    ALL_STATUSES,
    12→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    13→    scope_matches,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    16→    collapse_clusters,
    17→    enrich_plan_metadata,
    18→    filter_cluster_focus,
    19→    separate_skipped,
    20→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    21→    stamp_positions,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    24→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    27→    build_issue_items,
    28→    enrich_with_impact,
    29→    group_queue_items,
    30→    item_explain,
    31→    item_sort_key,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    34→    build_communicate_score_item,
    35→    build_create_plan_item,
    36→    build_import_scores_item,
    37→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    38→    build_subjective_items,
    39→    build_triage_stage_items,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    42→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    43→
    44→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    45→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    46→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:
    51→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    52→
    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    # Output control
    59→    count: int | None = 1
    60→    explain: bool = False
    61→
    62→    # Scope filtering
    63→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    64→    scope: str | None = None
    65→    status: str = "open"
    66→    chronic: bool = False
    67→
    68→    # Subjective gating
    69→    include_subjective: bool = True
    70→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   101→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   102→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   103→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   104→    """
   105→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   106→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   107→
   108→    # 1. Gather
   109→    items = build_issue_items(
   110→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   111→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   112→    )
   113→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   114→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   115→
   116→    # 2. Score & filter
   117→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   118→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   119→
   120→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   121→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   124→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   125→
   126→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   127→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   128→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   129→
   130→    # 6. Finalize
   131→    if not items:
   132→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   133→    total = len(items)
   134→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   135→        items = items[:opts.count]
   136→    if opts.explain:
   137→        for item in items:
   138→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   139→
   140→    return {
   141→        "items": items,
   142→        "total": total,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   144→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   145→    }
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→
   153→def _resolve_inputs(
   154→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   155→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   156→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   157→    ctx = opts.context
   158→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   159→
   160→    scan_path: str | None = (
   161→        state.get("scan_path")
   162→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   163→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    status = opts.status
   167→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   168→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   172→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   173→        threshold = 100.0
   174→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   175→
   176→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   177→
   178→
   179→def _gather_subjective_items(
   180→    state: StateModel,
   181→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   182→    threshold: float,
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   185→
   186→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   187→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   188→    """
   189→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   190→        return []
   191→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   192→        return []
   193→    if opts.chronic:
   194→        return []
   195→
   196→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   197→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   198→    )
   199→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   200→
   201→
   202→def _gather_workflow_items(
   203→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   204→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   205→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   206→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   210→    for builder in (
   211→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   212→        build_import_scores_item,
   213→        build_communicate_score_item,
   214→    ):
   215→        item = builder(plan, state)
   216→        if item is not None:
   217→            items.append(item)
   218→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   219→    if plan_item is not None:
   220→        items.append(plan_item)
   221→    return items
   222→
   223→
   224→_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   225→
   226→
   227→def _passes_impact_floor(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   228→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   229→    if item.get("kind") != "issue":
   230→        return True
   231→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   232→        return True
   233→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")
   234→    return not impact or impact >= _MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   235→
   236→
   237→def _plan_presort(
   238→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   239→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   240→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   241→
   242→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   243→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   244→    """
   245→    if not plan:
   246→        return set(), []
   247→
   248→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   249→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   250→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   251→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   252→    items[:] = remaining
   253→    return new_ids, skipped
   254→
   255→
   256→def _plan_postsort(
   257→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   258→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   259→    plan: dict | None,
   260→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   261→) -> None:
   262→    """Re-append skipped items, stamp positions, filter to cluster focus."""
   263→    if not plan:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    if opts.include_skipped:
   267→        items.extend(skipped)
   268→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   269→    focused = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, opts.cluster)
   270→    items[:] = focused
   271→
   272→
   273→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   274→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue."""
   275→    return any(
   276→        i.get("kind") == "issue"
   277→        and i.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   278→        for i in items
   279→    )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   283→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
   284→    return any(
   285→        i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   286→        and i.get("initial_review")
   287→        for i in items
   288→    )
   289→
   290→
   291→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   292→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
   293→    return (
   294→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   295→        and not item.get("initial_review")
   296→    )
   297→
   298→
   299→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   300→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   301→
   302→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → objective work.
   303→
   304→    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
   305→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   306→    2. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   307→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   308→    3. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   309→    """
   310→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
   311→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible — triage and workflow
   312→        # items stay hidden until initial reviews are complete.
   313→        return [
   314→            i for i in items
   315→            if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")
   316→        ]
   317→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   318→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   319→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   320→
   321→
   322→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   323→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   324→    if not plan:
   325→        return []
   326→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   327→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   328→        return []
   329→    return [{
   330→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   331→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   332→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   333→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   334→        "file": "",
   335→        "detector": "workflow",
   336→        "confidence": "high",
   337→    }]
   338→
   339→
   340→__all__ = [
   341→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   342→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   343→    "QueueContext",
   344→    "WorkQueueResult",
   345→    "build_work_queue",
   346→    "collapse_clusters",
   347→    "group_queue_items",
   348→]
   349→

    51→class TestAutoResolveOutOfScope:
    52→    def test_out_of_scope_issues_are_resolved(self):

    54→        existing = {

   251→        )
   252→        assert result["total"] == 2
   253→
   254→
   255→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→# Fix 3: compute_subjective_visibility respects scan_path + plan
   257→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   258→
   259→class TestSubjectivePolicyScanPathAndPlan:
   260→    def test_scan_path_excludes_out_of_scope_issues(self):
   261→        """Issues outside scan_path don't count toward objective backlog."""
   262→        state = _state_with_issues(
   263→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="src/app.ts"),
   264→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="supabase/fn.ts"),
   265→            _issue("f3", "unused", file="src/lib.ts"),
   266→        )
   267→        # Without scan_path: all 3 count
   268→        policy_all = compute_subjective_visibility(state)
   269→        assert policy_all.objective_count == 3
   270→
   271→        # With scan_path: only 2 in src/ count
   272→        policy_scoped = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src")
   273→        assert policy_scoped.objective_count == 2
   274→        assert policy_scoped.has_objective_backlog is True
   275→
   276→    def test_scan_path_drains_backlog_correctly(self):
   277→        """When all in-scope issues are resolved, backlog is drained even
   278→        if out-of-scope issues remain open."""
   279→        state = _state_with_issues(
   280→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="supabase/fn.ts"),
   281→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="supabase/other.ts"),
   282→        )
   283→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src")
   284→        assert policy.objective_count == 0
   285→        assert policy.has_objective_backlog is False
   286→
   287→    def test_plan_skipped_issues_excluded(self):
   288→        """Issues in plan['skipped'] don't count toward objective backlog."""
   289→        state = _state_with_issues(
   290→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="src/a.ts"),
   291→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="src/b.ts"),
   292→            _issue("f3", "unused", file="src/c.ts"),
   293→        )
   294→        plan = {"skipped": {"f1": {"kind": "temporary"}, "f2": {"kind": "permanent"}}}
   295→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan)
   296→        assert policy.objective_count == 1  # only f3
   297→
   298→    def test_scan_path_and_plan_combined(self):
   299→        """Both filters applied together: scope + skipped."""
   300→        state = _state_with_issues(
   301→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="src/a.ts"),     # in scope, not skipped → counts
   302→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="src/b.ts"),     # in scope, skipped → excluded
   303→            _issue("f3", "unused", file="other/c.ts"),   # out of scope → excluded
   304→        )
   305→        plan = {"skipped": {"f2": {"kind": "temporary"}}}
   306→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src", plan=plan)
   307→        assert policy.objective_count == 1
   308→        assert policy.has_objective_backlog is True
   309→
   310→    def test_no_params_backward_compat(self):
   311→        """Without new params, behavior is unchanged (all issues counted)."""
   312→        state = _state_with_issues(
   313→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="anywhere/a.ts"),
   314→        )
   315→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state)
   316→        assert policy.objective_count == 1
   317→

   322→
   323→class TestWorkflowRunScanItem:
   324→    def test_run_scan_item_injected_when_queue_empty_and_plan_active(self):
   325→        """When queue is empty and plan_start_scores exist, workflow::run-scan appears."""
   326→        from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
   327→            QueueBuildOptions,
   328→            build_work_queue,
   329→        )
   330→
   331→        state: dict = {"issues": {}, "scan_count": 5}

   333→            "plan_start_scores": {"strict": 75.0},
   334→            "queue_order": [],
   335→            "skipped": {},
   336→        }
   337→        result = build_work_queue(
   338→            state,
   339→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   340→                status="open",
   341→                count=None,
   342→                plan=plan,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→        items = result["items"]
   346→        assert len(items) == 1
   347→        assert items[0]["id"] == "workflow::run-scan"
   348→        assert items[0]["kind"] == "workflow_action"
   349→        assert "scan" in items[0]["primary_command"]
   350→

   352→        """When queue has real issues, no workflow::run-scan item."""

   358→        state: dict = {

   363→                    "summary": "test", "detail": {},

   378→                plan=plan,

   425→    def test_run_scan_does_not_block_scan_preflight(self):

   477→# Fix 6: queue_guard passes scan_path

   479→
   480→class TestQueueGuardScanPath:
   481→    def test_queue_guard_respects_scan_path_from_state(self):
   482→        """_check_queue_order_guard uses build_work_queue which auto-reads
   483→        scan_path from state, so out-of-scope items don't appear in the queue."""
   484→        from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
   485→
   486→        state = {
   487→            "issues": {
   488→                "f1": {"id": "f1", "status": "open", "file": "src/a.ts"},
   489→            },
   490→            "scan_path": "src",
   491→        }
   492→        mock_result = {
   493→            "total": 1,
   494→            "items": [{"id": "f1", "kind": "issue"}],
   495→            "grouped": {},
   496→            "new_ids": set(),
   497→        }
   498→        with (
   499→            patch("desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.has_living_plan", return_value=True),
   500→            patch("desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.load_plan", return_value={
   501→                "queue_order": ["f1"],
   502→                "skipped": {},
   503→            }),
   504→            patch(
   505→                "desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.build_work_queue",
   506→                return_value=mock_result,
   507→            ) as mock_build,
   508→        ):
   509→            # Should not raise — f1 is at front of queue
   510→            result = _check_queue_order_guard(state, ["f1"], "fixed")
   511→            assert result is False
   512→            # Verify build_work_queue was called (scan_path resolved internally)
   513→            mock_build.assert_called_once()
