1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import resurface_stale_skips
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    SupersededEntry,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    19→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    20→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    21→    sync_triage_needed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    changes: int = 0
    36→
    37→
    38→def _find_candidates(
    39→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    40→) -> list[str]:
    41→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    42→    candidates: list[str] = []
    43→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    44→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    45→            continue
    46→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    47→            candidates.append(fid)
    48→    return candidates
    49→
    50→
    51→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    53→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    54→    if issue is None:
    55→        return False
    56→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    57→
    58→
    59→def _supersede_id(
    60→    plan: PlanModel,
    61→    state: StateModel,
    62→    issue_id: str,
    63→    now: str,
    64→) -> bool:
    65→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    66→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    67→    detector = ""
    68→    file = ""
    69→    summary = ""
    70→    if issue:
    71→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    72→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    73→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    74→
    75→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    76→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    77→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    78→
    79→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    80→        "original_id": issue_id,
    81→        "original_detector": detector,
    82→        "original_file": file,
    83→        "original_summary": summary,
    84→        "status": "superseded",
    85→        "superseded_at": now,
    86→        "remapped_to": None,
    87→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    # Preserve any existing override note
    91→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    92→    if override_note:
    93→        entry["note"] = override_note
    94→
    95→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    96→
    97→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    98→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    99→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   100→    if issue_id in order:
   101→        order.remove(issue_id)
   102→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   103→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   104→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   105→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   106→        if issue_id in ids:
   107→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   108→
   109→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   110→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   111→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   112→        override["cluster"] = None
   113→        override["updated_at"] = now
   114→
   115→    return True
   116→
   117→
   118→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   119→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   120→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   121→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   122→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   123→
   124→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   125→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   126→        try:
   127→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   128→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   129→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   130→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   131→                to_prune.append(fid)
   132→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   133→            to_prune.append(fid)
   134→
   135→    for fid in to_prune:
   136→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   137→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   138→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   139→
   140→    return to_prune
   141→
   142→
   143→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   144→    plan: PlanModel,
   145→    state: StateModel,
   146→) -> ReconcileResult:
   147→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   148→
   149→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   150→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   151→    """
   152→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   153→    result = ReconcileResult()
   154→    now = utc_now()
   155→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   156→
   157→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   158→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   159→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   161→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   162→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   163→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   164→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   165→
   166→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   167→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   168→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   169→    referenced_ids = {
   170→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   171→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    # Check each referenced ID
   175→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   176→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   177→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   178→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   179→                result.changes += 1
   180→
   181→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   182→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   183→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   184→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   185→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   186→            continue
   187→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   188→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   189→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   190→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   191→            result.changes += 1
   192→        if not alive_ids:
   193→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   194→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   195→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   196→        result.changes += 1
   197→
   198→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   199→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   200→
   201→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   202→    if resurfaced:
   203→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   204→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   205→
   206→    # Prune old superseded entries
   207→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   208→    result.pruned = pruned
   209→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   210→
   211→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   212→    if result.changes > 0:
   213→        append_log_entry(
   214→            plan,
   215→            "reconcile",
   216→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   217→            actor="system",
   218→            detail={
   219→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   220→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   221→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   222→            },
   223→        )
   224→
   225→    return result
   226→
   227→
   228→@dataclass
   229→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   230→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   231→
   232→    new_ids: set[str]
   233→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   234→    triage_injected: bool
   235→
   236→
   237→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   238→    plan: PlanModel,
   239→    state: StateModel,
   240→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   241→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   242→
   243→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   244→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new issues to sync.
   245→    """
   246→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   247→    new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   248→    if not new_ids:
   249→        return None
   250→
   251→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   252→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   253→    existing = set(order)
   254→    added: list[str] = []
   255→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   256→        if fid not in existing:
   257→            order.append(fid)
   258→            added.append(fid)
   259→
   260→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   261→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   262→    triage_injected = bool(
   263→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   264→    )
   265→
   266→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   267→        new_ids=new_ids,
   268→        added_to_queue=added,
   269→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   270→    )
   271→
   272→
   273→__all__ = [
   274→    "ReconcileResult",
   275→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   276→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   277→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   278→]
   279→

   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------

341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   350→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   351→
   352→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   353→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   354→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   355→    """
   356→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   357→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   358→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   359→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   360→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   361→
   362→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   363→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   364→
   365→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   366→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   367→
   368→    if already_present:
   369→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   370→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   371→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   372→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   373→        # user-started triage session.
   374→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   375→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   376→
   377→            if not new_since_triage:
   378→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   379→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   380→                if current_hash:
   381→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→                result.pruned = True
   384→        return result
   385→
   386→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   387→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   388→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   389→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   390→
   391→        if new_since_triage:
   392→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   393→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   394→        else:
   395→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   396→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   397→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   398→
   399→    return result
   400→
   401→

   403→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   405→
   406→    injected: bool = False
   407→
   408→    @property
   409→    def changes(self) -> int:
   410→        return int(self.injected)
   411→
   412→
   413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result

465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→
   518→
   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   525→
   526→
   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)

601→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   602→    plan: PlanModel,
   603→    state: StateModel,
   604→    *,
   605→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   606→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   607→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   608→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   609→
   610→    Injects when either:
   611→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   612→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   613→
   614→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   615→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   616→    """
   617→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   618→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   619→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   620→
   621→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   622→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   623→        return result
   624→
   625→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   626→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   627→
   628→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   629→    if not should_inject:
   630→        if policy is not None:
   631→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   632→        else:
   633→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   634→
   635→    if not should_inject:
   636→        return result
   637→
   638→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   639→    insert_at = 0
   640→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   641→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   642→            insert_at = i + 1
   643→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   644→    result.injected = True
   645→    return result
   646→
   647→
   648→__all__ = [
   649→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   650→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   651→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   652→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   653→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   654→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   655→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   656→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   657→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   658→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   659→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
   660→    "CommunicateScoreSyncResult",

## 1. What a realistic state looks like after scan

### Top-level keys in a real `state-python.json`

## 2. How subjective reviews work: `subjective_assessments` and `dimension_scores`

### After a real review import — `subjective_assessments`

A freshly scored (non-stale) assessment:

```python
# state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"]
{
    "score": 65.0,
    "source": "holistic",
    "assessed_at": "2026-03-04T03:46:43+00:00",
}
```

A stale assessment (triggered when `mechanical_issues_changed`):

```python
# state["subjective_assessments"]["abstraction_fitness"]
{
    "score": 48.1,
    "source": "holistic",
    "assessed_at": "2026-03-04T03:46:26+00:00",
    "components": ["Abstraction Leverage", "Indirection Cost", ...],
    "component_scores": {"Abstraction Leverage": 70.0, ...},
    "needs_review_refresh": True,
    "refresh_reason": "mechanical_issues_changed",
    "stale_since": "2026-03-04T16:09:18+00:00",
}
```

A placeholder (never reviewed) assessment:

```python

    "score": 0.0,
    "placeholder": True,
}
```

### `dimension_scores` — real data vs placeholder

A scored dimension:

```python
# state["dimension_scores"]["Naming quality"]
{
    "score": 65.0,
    "strict": 65.0,
    "verified_strict_score": 65.0,
    "checks": 10,
    "failing": 0,
    "tier": 4,
    "detectors": {
        "subjective_assessment": {
            "potential": 10,
            "pass_rate": 0.65,
            "failing": 0,
            "weighted_failures": 3.5,
            "assessment_score": 65.0,
            "placeholder": False,
            "dimension_key": "naming_quality",
            "configured_weight": 2.0,
            "components": [],
        }
    },
}
```

A placeholder dimension (never reviewed):

```python
# state["dimension_scores"]["Naming quality"]  (placeholder scenario)
{
    "score": 0.0,
    "strict": 0.0,
    "failing": 0,
    "detectors": {
        "subjective_assessment": {
            "dimension_key": "naming_quality",
            "placeholder": True,
        }
    },
}
```

The `placeholder` flag in the `detectors.subjective_assessment` sub-dict is what the queue lifecycle filter keyed as "initial reviews" gating. The `stale` flag in `dimension_scores` (the top-level dict entry, not in `detectors`) matters for the stale-dimension sync.

---

## 3. How `plan.json` is structured

`empty_plan()` (`desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`) gives you the full v7 shape:

```python
{
    "version": 7,
    "created": "<now>",
    "updated": "<now>",
    "queue_order": [],                # THE ordering list — all item IDs in priority order
    "deferred": [],                   # legacy/empty compat field
    "skipped": {},                    # {issue_id: SkipEntry}
    "active_cluster": None,           # name of focused cluster or None
    "overrides": {},                  # {issue_id: ItemOverride}
    "clusters": {},                   # {cluster_name: Cluster}
    "superseded": {},                 # {old_issue_id: SupersededEntry}
    "promoted_ids": [],               # IDs explicitly positioned by user
    "plan_start_scores": {},          # frozen at start of cycle: {"strict": 85.0, ...}
    "execution_log": [],              # [{timestamp, action, issue_ids, actor, ...}]
    "epic_triage_meta": {},           # triage stage progress tracker
    "commit_log": [],
    "uncommitted_issues": [],
    "commit_tracking_branch": None,
}
```

### What `queue_order` actually contains

`queue_order` is a list of string IDs. These can be:

- **Objective issue IDs**: `"unused::src/a.ts::foo"`, `"structural::src/b.py"`, etc.
- **Subjective dimension IDs**: `"subjective::naming_quality"`, `"subjective::cross_module_architecture"`
- **Workflow action IDs**: `"workflow::communicate-score"`, `"workflow::create-plan"`, `"workflow::score-checkpoint"`
- **Triage stage IDs**: `"triage::observe"`, `"triage::reflect"`, `"triage::organize"`, `"triage::commit"`

After triage is triggered, the real plan's `queue_order` looks like:

```python
["triage::observe", "triage::reflect", "triage::organize", "triage::commit"]
```

### A realistic populated plan after triage:

```python
{
    "version": 7,
    "queue_order": [
        "triage::observe",
        "triage::reflect",
        "triage::organize",
        "triage::commit",
        "unused::src/api.ts::authHelper",
        "structural::src/services/UserService.ts",

    "overrides": {

            "issue_id": "unused::src/api.ts::authHelper",

        }
    },

        {"timestamp": "...", "action": "sync_communicate_score", "actor": "system", "detail": {"injected": True}},
        {"timestamp": "...", "action": "reconcile", "actor": "system", "detail": {...}},
    ],
    ...
}
```

---

## 4. How reconcile injects workflow items

`reconcile_plan_post_scan()` in `/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` runs after every scan and orchestrates these steps in order:

1. **`_apply_plan_reconciliation`** — Supersedes (moves to `superseded`) any `queue_order`/`skipped`/`overrides`/cluster-member IDs that no longer exist in state or are no longer `status="open"`. Returns surfaced skips. Only runs when plan has user content (non-empty `queue_order`, `overrides`, `clusters`, or `skipped`).

2. **`_sync_unscored_and_log`** — Calls `sync_unscored_dimensions`: prepends `subjective::<slug>` IDs to the **front** of `queue_order` for any dimension that is still a placeholder (never scored). This is onboarding priority.

3. **`_sync_stale_and_log`** — Calls `sync_stale_dimensions`: prunes already-refreshed subjective IDs from `queue_order`, and appends stale (needs re-review) subjective IDs to the back when no objective backlog exists.

4. **`_sync_auto_clusters_and_log`** — Regenerates auto-created clusters.

5. **`_sync_communicate_score_and_log`** — Injects `workflow::communicate-score` when:
   - No placeholder (unscored) dimensions remain, AND
   - `workflow::communicate-score` is not already in `queue_order`

6. **`_sync_create_plan_and_log`** — Injects `workflow::create-plan` when:
   - No unscored dimensions remain, AND
   - Objective backlog exists, AND
   - No triage stages pending, AND
   - Not already in queue

7. **`_sync_triage_and_log`** — Injects `triage::observe`, `triage::reflect`, `triage::organize`, `triage::commit` (the four stages) when new review/concerns issues appeared since the last triage hash.

8. **`_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log`** — Seeds `plan_start_scores` on first scan of a new cycle (when `plan_start_scores == {}`), or clears it when the queue is drained.

**Only saves once** — dirty flag accumulates, single `save_plan()` call at end.

## 5. Existing test helpers and fixtures

### In `tests/review/work_queue_cases.py`

These are the best minimal state helpers that test queue behavior:

```python
def _issue(
    fid: str,
    *,
    detector: str = "smells",
    file: str = "src/a.py",
    tier: int = 3,
    confidence: str = "medium",
    status: str = "open",
    detail: dict | None = None,
) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": fid,
        "detector": detector,
        "file": file,
        "tier": tier,
        "confidence": confidence,
        "summary": fid,
        "status": status,
        "detail": detail or {},
    }

def _state(issues: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    return {
        "issues": {f["id"]: f for f in issues},
        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    }
```

This minimal state is sufficient for `build_work_queue()`.

### In `tests/commands/scan/test_plan_reconcile.py`

```python
def _runtime(*, state=None, config=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    return SimpleNamespace(
        state=state or {},
        state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"),
        config=config or {},
    )

def _make_state(
    *,
    issues: dict | None = None,
    overall_score: float | None = None,
    objective_score: float | None = None,
    strict_score: float | None = None,
    verified_strict_score: float | None = None,
    scan_count: int = 1,
) -> dict:
    state: dict = {
        "issues": issues or {},
        "scan_count": scan_count,
        "dimension_scores": {},
        "subjective_assessments": {},
    }
    # optionals added only when not None
    ...

def _make_issue(
    detector: str = "complexity",
    file: str = "src/app.py",
    status: str = "open",
    **extra,
) -> dict:
    return {"detector": detector, "file": file, "status": status, **extra}
```

## 6. Minimum state shape for each lifecycle stage

### Stage 1: Post-scan, pre-review (fresh project, no assessments)

```python
state = {
    "scan_count": 1,
    "strict_score": 0.0,
    "objective_score": 82.0,
    "overall_score": 0.0,
    "verified_strict_score": 0.0,
    "issues": {
        "unused::src/a.ts::foo": {"id": "unused::src/a.ts::foo", "detector": "unused",
                                   "file": "src/a.ts", "tier": 1, "confidence": "high",
                                   "summary": "...", "detail": {}, "status": "open"},
    },
    "dimension_scores": {
        "Naming quality": {
            "score": 0.0, "strict": 0.0, "failing": 0,
            "detectors": {
                "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality", "placeholder": True}
            },
        },
    },
    "subjective_assessments": {
        "naming_quality": {"score": 0.0, "placeholder": True}
    },
}
```

Reconcile will: inject `subjective::naming_quality` at front of `queue_order`.

### Stage 2: After initial reviews (assessments set, objective backlog remains)

```python
state = {
    "scan_count": 3,
    "strict_score": 65.0,
    "objective_score": 88.0,
    "overall_score": 65.0,
    "verified_strict_score": 65.0,
    "issues": {
        "unused::src/a.ts::foo": {"id": "...", "detector": "unused", "status": "open", ...},
    },
    "dimension_scores": {
        "Naming quality": {
            "score": 65.0, "strict": 65.0, "verified_strict_score": 65.0,
            "checks": 10, "failing": 0, "tier": 4,
            "detectors": {
                "subjective_assessment": {
                    "dimension_key": "naming_quality", "placeholder": False,
                    "assessment_score": 65.0, "pass_rate": 0.65,
                    "weighted_failures": 3.5, "potential": 10,
                }
            },
        },
    },
    "subjective_assessments": {
        "naming_quality": {"score": 65.0, "source": "holistic", "assessed_at": "2026-03-04T03:46:43+00:00"},
    },
}
```

Reconcile will: inject `workflow::communicate-score` then `workflow::create-plan`.

### Stage 3: After reviews, with new review issues (triage needed)

```python
state["issues"]["review::.::holistic::naming_quality::some-slug::abc12345"] = {
    "id": "review::.::holistic::naming_quality::some-slug::abc12345",
    "detector": "review",
    "file": ".",
    "tier": 1, "confidence": "high",
    "summary": "...",
    "detail": {"holistic": True, "dimension": "naming_quality"},
    "status": "open",
}
```

With `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["issue_snapshot_hash"]` not matching the current hash, reconcile will inject the 4 triage stages.

### Stage 4: Full plan cycle (queue_order populated, plan_start_scores set)

```python
plan = {
    "version": 7, "created": "...", "updated": "...",
    "queue_order": ["unused::src/a.ts::foo", "workflow::communicate-score"],
    "plan_start_scores": {"strict": 65.0, "overall": 65.0, "objective": 88.0, "verified": 65.0},
    "overrides": {"unused::src/a.ts::foo": {"issue_id": "unused::src/a.ts::foo"}},
    "skipped": {}, "clusters": {}, "superseded": {},
    "promoted_ids": [], "deferred": [], "active_cluster": None,
    "execution_log": [], "epic_triage_meta": {}, "commit_log": [],
    "uncommitted_issues": [], "commit_tracking_branch": None,
}
```

---

## 7. Key things reconcile expects

- **`state["issues"]`** — a dict keyed by ID, each with at least `"status"` and `"detector"` fields.
- **`state["scan_count"]`** — int, used to resurface temporary skips.
- **`state["dimension_scores"]`** — dict keyed by display name (e.g., `"Naming quality"`). Inner shape: `{"score", "strict", "checks", "failing", "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": bool, "dimension_key": str}}}`.
- **`state["subjective_assessments"]`** — dict keyed by slug (e.g., `"naming_quality"`). Inner shape: `{"score", "placeholder"?}` for placeholder, or `{"score", "source", "assessed_at", "needs_review_refresh"?, "stale_since"?}` for real data.
- **`plan["queue_order"]`** — list of string IDs (all types mixed together).
- **`plan["epic_triage_meta"]["issue_snapshot_hash"]`** — SHA-256-based hash of open review issue IDs. Absent or stale hash triggers triage injection.

### How the snapshot hash is computed

```python
# desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py, line ~330ish
def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state):
    ids = sorted(
        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
        if f.get("status") == "open"
        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16] if ids else ""
```

---

## 8. How `build_work_queue` uses the plan

The pipeline in `build_work_queue()` (`desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`):

1. **Gather** issues + subjective items (from `dimension_scores`) + workflow items (from plan `queue_order`)
2. **Score** with `enrich_with_impact` using `dimension_scores` headroom
3. **Presort**: `stamp_plan_sort_keys` applies plan `queue_order` positions as sort keys; `separate_skipped` removes `plan["skipped"]` items
4. **Lifecycle filter** (`_apply_lifecycle_filter`):
   - Phase 1: if any `initial_review=True` subjective items exist → only show those
   - Phase 2: if objective items exist → hide endgame-only subjective (stale re-reviews)
   - Phase 3: nothing objective → everything visible
5. **Sort** by `item_sort_key`; apply plan position stamps
6. **Collapse clusters**, stamp positions
7. **Truncate** to `count`

The `QueueBuildOptions` defaults: `count=1`, `include_subjective=True`, `subjective_threshold=100.0`, `status="open"`.

---

## 9. Summary: What a minimal integration test state factory needs

For a test exercising `scan → subjective review → score → triage`:

```python
from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
from desloppify.state import empty_state

def make_post_scan_state(*, placeholder_dims=True, has_objective=True):
    """State right after scan — no reviews yet."""
    state = empty_state()
    state["scan_count"] = 1
    state["strict_score"] = 0.0
    state["objective_score"] = 88.0
    if has_objective:
        state["issues"]["unused::src/a.ts::foo"] = {
            "id": "unused::src/a.ts::foo", "detector": "unused",
            "file": "src/a.ts", "tier": 1, "confidence": "high",
            "summary": "Unused: foo", "detail": {}, "status": "open",
        }
    if placeholder_dims:
        state["dimension_scores"]["Naming quality"] = {
            "score": 0.0, "strict": 0.0, "failing": 0,
            "detectors": {
                "subjective_assessment": {
                    "dimension_key": "naming_quality", "placeholder": True
                }
            },
        }
        state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"] = {
            "score": 0.0, "placeholder": True
        }
    return state

def make_reviewed_state(*, score=65.0, stale=False):
    """State after review imports (assessments exist)."""
    state = make_post_scan_state(placeholder_dims=False)
    state["strict_score"] = score
    state["overall_score"] = score
    state["dimension_scores"]["Naming quality"] = {
        "score": score, "strict": score, "checks": 10, "failing": 0, "tier": 4,
        "detectors": {
            "subjective_assessment": {
                "dimension_key": "naming_quality", "placeholder": False,
                "assessment_score": score, "pass_rate": score / 100,
                "weighted_failures": (100 - score) / 10,
            }
        },
    }
    entry = {"score": score, "source": "holistic", "assessed_at": "2026-03-04T00:00:00+00:00"}
    if stale:
        entry["needs_review_refresh"] = True
        entry["stale_since"] = "2026-03-04T16:00:00+00:00"
    state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"] = entry
    return state
```

The key pattern throughout all existing tests is: **mock at I/O boundaries** (`load_plan`/`save_plan`/`load_state`), use `empty_plan()` and hand-crafted state dicts, and call the real engine functions directly. The `_make_state` + `_make_issue` pattern in `test_plan_reconcile.py` and the `_issue` + `_state` helpers in `work_queue_cases.py` are the canonical models for the test suite.

   382→_BASE_DETECTORS: dict[str, DetectorMeta] = dict(DETECTORS)
   383→_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER: list[str] = list(DISPLAY_ORDER)
   384→_BASE_JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   385→    name for name, meta in _BASE_DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   386→)
   387→
   388→
   389→@dataclass
   390→class _RegistryRuntime:
   391→    detectors: dict[str, DetectorMeta]
   392→    display_order: list[str]
   393→    callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]]
   394→    judgment_detectors: frozenset[str]
   395→
   396→
   397→_RUNTIME = _RegistryRuntime(
   398→    detectors=DETECTORS,
   399→    display_order=list(DISPLAY_ORDER),
   400→    callbacks=[],
   401→    judgment_detectors=frozenset(
   402→        name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   403→    ),
   404→)
   405→
   406→# Compatibility handles kept for existing imports/tests.
   407→DETECTORS = _RUNTIME.detectors
   408→_DISPLAY_ORDER = _RUNTIME.display_order
   409→_on_register_callbacks = _RUNTIME.callbacks
   410→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = _RUNTIME.judgment_detectors
   411→
   412→
   413→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   414→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   415→    _RUNTIME.callbacks.append(callback)
   416→
   417→
   418→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   419→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   420→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   421→    _RUNTIME.detectors[meta.name] = meta
   422→    if meta.name not in _RUNTIME.display_order:
   423→        _RUNTIME.display_order.append(meta.name)
   424→    _RUNTIME.judgment_detectors = frozenset(
   425→        name for name, m in _RUNTIME.detectors.items() if m.needs_judgment
   426→    )
   427→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = _RUNTIME.judgment_detectors
   428→    for cb in tuple(_RUNTIME.callbacks):
   429→        cb()
   430→
   431→
   432→def reset_registered_detectors() -> None:
   433→    """Reset runtime-added detector registrations to built-in defaults."""
   434→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   435→    _RUNTIME.detectors.clear()
   436→    _RUNTIME.detectors.update(_BASE_DETECTORS)
   437→    _RUNTIME.display_order.clear()
   438→    _RUNTIME.display_order.extend(_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   439→    _RUNTIME.judgment_detectors = _BASE_JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   440→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = _RUNTIME.judgment_detectors
   441→    for cb in tuple(_RUNTIME.callbacks):
   442→        cb()
   443→
   444→
   445→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   446→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   447→    return sorted(_RUNTIME.detectors.keys())
   448→
   449→
   450→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   451→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   452→    return list(_RUNTIME.display_order)
   453→
   454→
   455→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   456→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   457→    "auto_fix": "autofix",
   458→    "reorganize": "move",
   459→    "refactor": "refactor",
   460→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   461→}
   462→
   463→
   464→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   465→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   466→    best = "manual"
   467→    best_pri = 99
   468→    for d in _RUNTIME.detectors.values():
   469→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   470→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   471→            if pri < best_pri:
   472→                best_pri = pri
   473→                best = d.action_type
   474→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   475→
   476→
   477→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   478→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   479→    result = {}
   480→    for name, d in _RUNTIME.detectors.items():
   481→        entry: dict = {
   482→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   483→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   484→        }
   485→        if d.tool:
   486→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   487→        if d.guidance:
   488→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   489→        result[name] = entry
   490→    return result
   491→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    19→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   250→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   251→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   252→
   253→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   254→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   260→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   261→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→
   263→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   264→    plan: PlanModel,
   265→    state: StateModel,
   266→    *,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   270→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   271→
   272→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   273→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   274→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   275→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   276→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   277→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   278→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   279→    """
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   282→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   283→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   284→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   285→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   286→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   287→
   288→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   289→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   290→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   291→
   292→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   293→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   294→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   295→
   296→    if not should_inject:
   297→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   298→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   299→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   300→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   301→        to_evict = [
   302→            fid for fid in order
   303→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   304→            and fid in injectable_ids
   305→        ]
   306→        for fid in to_evict:
   307→            order.remove(fid)
   308→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   309→
   310→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   311→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   312→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   313→
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→
   321→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   322→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   323→
   324→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   325→    """
   326→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   327→
   328→
   329→@dataclass
   330→class TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   332→
   333→    injected: bool = False
   334→    pruned: bool = False
   335→
   336→    @property
   337→    def changes(self) -> int:
   338→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   339→
   340→
   341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   350→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   351→
   352→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   353→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   354→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   355→    """
   356→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   357→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   358→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   359→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   360→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   361→
   362→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   363→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   364→
   365→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   366→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   367→
   368→    if already_present:
   369→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   370→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   371→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   372→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   373→        # user-started triage session.
   374→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   375→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   376→
   377→            if not new_since_triage:
   378→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   379→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   380→                if current_hash:
   381→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→                result.pruned = True
   384→        return result
   385→
   386→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   387→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   388→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   389→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   390→
   391→        if new_since_triage:
   392→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   393→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   394→        else:
   395→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   396→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   397→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   398→
   399→    return result
   400→
   401→
   402→@dataclass
   403→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   405→
   406→    injected: bool = False
   407→
   408→    @property
   409→    def changes(self) -> int:
   410→        return int(self.injected)
   411→
   412→
   413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result
   452→
   453→
   454→@dataclass
   455→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   456→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   457→
   458→    injected: bool = False
   459→
   460→    @property
   461→    def changes(self) -> int:
   462→        return int(self.injected)
   463→
   464→
   465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→
   518→
   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   525→
   526→
   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   541→
   542→
   543→@dataclass
   544→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   545→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   546→
   547→    injected: bool = False
   548→
   549→    @property
   550→    def changes(self) -> int:
   551→        return int(self.injected)
   552→
   553→
   554→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   555→    plan: PlanModel,
   556→    state: StateModel,
   557→    *,
   558→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   559→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   560→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   561→
   562→    Only injects when:
   563→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   564→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   565→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   566→
   567→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   568→    """
   569→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   570→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   571→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   572→
   573→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   574→        return result
   575→
   576→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   577→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   578→        return result
   579→
   580→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   581→    insert_at = 0
   582→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   583→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   584→            insert_at = i + 1
   585→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   586→    result.injected = True
   587→    return result
   588→
   589→
   590→@dataclass
   591→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   592→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   593→
   594→    injected: bool = False
   595→
   596→    @property
   597→    def changes(self) -> int:
   598→        return int(self.injected)
   599→
   600→
   601→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   602→    plan: PlanModel,
   603→    state: StateModel,
   604→    *,
   605→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   606→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   607→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   608→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   609→
   610→    Injects when either:
   611→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   612→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   613→
   614→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   615→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   616→    """
   617→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   618→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   619→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   620→
   621→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   622→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   623→        return result
   624→
   625→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   626→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   627→
   628→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   629→    if not should_inject:
   630→        if policy is not None:
   631→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   632→        else:
   633→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   634→
   635→    if not should_inject:
   636→        return result
   637→
   638→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   639→    insert_at = 0
   640→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   641→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   642→            insert_at = i + 1
   643→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   644→    result.injected = True
   645→    return result
   646→
   647→
   648→__all__ = [
   649→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   650→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   651→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   652→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   653→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   654→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
