## 5. `build_work_queue` — How Workflow Items Are Constructed

**Source**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 90–145, with workflow item building at lines 204–221.

The pipeline in `build_work_queue`:
1. **Gather**: `build_issue_items()` for objective issues, `_gather_subjective_items()` for subjective dimensions, `_gather_workflow_items()` for triage/checkpoint/create-plan items.
2. **Score & filter**: `enrich_with_impact()` then drop items below `_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05` (but `kind != "issue"` items pass unconditionally, as do review/subjective issues).
3. **Plan presort**: separate skipped items, stamp plan sort keys.
4. **Lifecycle filter**: `_apply_lifecycle_filter()`.
5. **Sort**: `item_sort_key()`.
6. **Finalize**: truncate to `count`, optionally add explain metadata.

`_gather_workflow_items` (lines 204–221):

```python
def _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status):
    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
        return []

    items = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
    for builder in (
        build_score_checkpoint_item,
        build_import_scores_item,
        build_communicate_score_item,
    ):
        item = builder(plan, state)
        if item is not None:
            items.append(item)
    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
    if plan_item is not None:
        items.append(plan_item)
    return items
```

Requires a non-None `plan` and `status` in `{"open", "all"}`.

### `build_score_checkpoint_item` (synthetic_workflow.py lines 8–39)

Checks: `WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in plan.get("queue_order", [])`. If not, returns `None`.

When present, reads `state_mod.score_snapshot(state)` for the current strict score, reads `plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]` for the baseline, computes delta. Returns a `WorkQueueItem` with:
- `id`: `"workflow::score-checkpoint"`
- `kind`: `"workflow_action"`, `tier`: 1, `confidence`: `"high"`, `detector`: `"workflow"`, `file`: `"."`
- `summary`: `"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}"`
- `detail`: `{strict, plan_start_strict, delta}`
- `primary_command`: `desloppify plan resolve "workflow::score-checkpoint" --note "Reviewed score checkpoint" --confirm`

### `build_create_plan_item` (synthetic_workflow.py lines 42–61)

Checks: `WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", [])`. If not, returns `None`.

Returns a static `WorkQueueItem`:
- `id`: `"workflow::create-plan"`
- `kind`: `"workflow_action"`, `tier`: 1, `confidence`: `"high"`, `detector`: `"workflow"`, `file`: `"."`
- `summary`: `"Create prioritized plan from review results"`
- `detail`: `{}`
- `primary_command`: `desloppify plan resolve "workflow::create-plan" --note "Plan reviewed and queue organized" --confirm`

Both items have `blocked_by: []` and `is_blocked: False`. Both are purely presence-gated on `queue_order` — they do not re-evaluate eligibility conditions at build time; that was done at sync time when the IDs were inserted.

---

## 6. `_has_initial_reviews` — Exact Implementation

**Source**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 282–288.

```python
def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
    return any(
        i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
        and i.get("initial_review")
        for i in items
    )
```

It checks whether any item in the already-built item list has `kind == "subjective_dimension"` AND `initial_review` truthy. The `initial_review` flag is set on subjective items representing placeholder (never-scored) dimensions — dimensions that have never had any assessment run. This flag comes from `build_subjective_items` in the synthetic module, which sets `initial_review=True` for unscored/placeholder dimensions.

This function gates the lifecycle filter (lines 310–318):

```python
if _has_initial_reviews(items):
    # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible
    return [
        i for i in items
        if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")
    ]
```

When any initial reviews are present, ALL other items (objective issues, triage stages, workflow checkpoints) are hidden. Only the initial-review subjective dimensions are shown.
